CREATE TABLE RESTAURANT_TABLE(
     TableNo NUMBER(3) NOT NULL,
     Capacity NUMBER(2) CHECK (Capacity > 0),
     LOCATION VARCHAR(50),
     PRIMARY KEY (TableNo)
);

CREATE TABLE MENU (
     MenuId VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
     MenuName VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
     MenuDesc VARCHAR(50),
     MenuPrice NUMBER(3,2) DEFAULT 0 ,
     PRIMARY KEY(MenuId)
);

CREATE TABLE  PROMOTIONAL_SET(
     PromoId VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
     PromoName VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
     PromoDesc VARCHAR2(50),
     PromoPrice NUMBER(4,2),
     PRIMARY KEY(PromoId)
);

CREATE TABLE SET_MENU(
     SetMenuId VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
     SetName VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
     SetDesc VARCHAR(50),
     SetPrice NUMBER(4,2),
     PRIMARY KEY(SetMenuId)
);

CREATE TABLE STAFF (
     StaffId VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
     StaffName VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
     StaffPhone VARCHAR(11),
     DateJoined DATE DEFAULT SYSDATE,
     PRIMARY KEY (StaffId)
);

CREATE TABLE SUPPLIER (
     SupplierId VARCHAR2(10) NOT NULL,
     SupplierName VARCHAR(50),
     SupplierPhone VARCHAR(11),
     PRIMARY KEY (SupplierId)
);

CREATE TABLE CANCELLATION (
     CancelId VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
     CancelDate DATE DEFAULT SYSDATE,
     OrderNo VARCHAR(10),
     PRIMARY KEY (CancelId),
     FOREIGN KEY (OrderNo) REFERENCES FOOD_ORDER(OrderNo)
);

CREATE TABLE DELIVERY(
     DeliveryNo NUMBER(10) NOT NULL,
     DeliveryContact VARCHAR(11),
     DeliveryPartner VARCHAR(10),
     OrderNo VARCHAR(10),
     PRIMARY KEY (DeliveryNo),
     FOREIGN KEY (OrderNo) REFERENCES FOOD_ORDER(OrderNo)
);

CREATE TABLE INGREDIENT(
     IngId VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
     IngName VARCHAR(20),
     IngDesc VARCHAR(50),
     Unit VARCHAR(10),
     Balance NUMBER DEFAULT 0,
     UpdtDate DATE DEFAULT SYSDATE,
     SupplierId VARCHAR(10),
     PRIMARY KEY(IngId),
     FOREIGN KEY (SupplierId) REFERENCES SUPPLIER(SupplierId)
);

CREATE TABLE FOOD_ORDER(
     OrderNo VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
     OrderDate DATE DEFAULT SYSDATE,
     EatMethod VARCHAR(2) CHECK (EatMethod IN ('DI', 'TA')),
     NoOfCust NUMBER,
     TotalItems NUMBER,
     FoodAmount NUMBER,
     SST NUMBER(5,2),
     ServiceCharge NUMBER(5,2),
     GrandTotal NUMBER(5,2),
     TableNo NUMBER(3),
     StaffId VARCHAR(10),
     PRIMARY KEY (OrderNo),
     FOREIGN KEY (TableNo) REFERENCES RESTAURANT_TABLE(TableNo),
     FOREIGN KEY (StaffId) REFERENCES STAFF(StaffId)
);

CREATE TABLE INGREDIENT_LIST(
     IngId VARCHAR(10),
     MenuId VARCHAR(10),
     UnitUsed VARCHAR(10),
     QtyUsed NUMBER(5,2) CHECK (QtyUsed > 0),
     PRIMARY KEY (IngId, MenuId),
     FOREIGN KEY (IngId) REFERENCES INGREDIENT(IngId),
     FOREIGN KEY (MenuId) REFERENCES MENU(MenuId)
);

CREATE TABLE ORDER_LIST (
     OrderNo VARCHAR(10),
     MenuId VARCHAR(10),
     ItemQty NUMBER CHECK (ItemQty > 0),
     Price NUMBER(5,2),
     PRIMARY KEY (OrderNo, MenuId),
     FOREIGN KEY (OrderNo) REFERENCES FOOD_ORDER(OrderNo),
     FOREIGN KEY (MenuId) REFERENCES MENU(MenuId)
);

CREATE TABLE  PROMOTIONAL_SET_ITEM (
     PromoId VARCHAR(10),
     MenuId VARCHAR(10),
     Qty NUMBER,
     PRIMARY KEY (PromoId, MenuId),
     FOREIGN KEY (PromoId) REFERENCES PROMOTIONAL_SET(PromoId),
     FOREIGN KEY (MenuId) REFERENCES MENU(MenuId)
);

CREATE TABLE SET_MENU_ITEM (
     SetMenuId VARCHAR(10),
     MenuId VARCHAR(10),
     Qty NUMBER,
     PRIMARY KEY (SetMenuId, MenuId),
     FOREIGN KEY (SetMenuId) REFERENCES SET_MENU(SetMenuId),
     FOREIGN KEY (MenuId) REFERENCES MENU(MenuId)
);
